Where the action itself repaints a state — the 深度 select's selected option, the risk panel's 全部放行/条件放行 pill moving to the clicked one, a list row appearing or disappearing, the composer holding the prefilled text after a Fork, the settings line already reading `当前 vN · 已是最新` — the success toast restates the visible and is not written. Failure always speaks: that branch changes nothing on screen, so silence reads as success. Two traps when applying this: a success toast that follows its own `persistent: true` progress toast inside the same function is that sticky layer's only dismissal, so deleting it parks the progress overlay on screen until the next unrelated toast — retire both together or leave both; and a call carrying `kind: "success"` with `persistent: true` and an in-progress title (`检测连接中`, `Saving`) is a busy indicator borrowing the green skin, not a terminal receipt, so it is not a redundancy candidate. A third kind sits outside the rule in the other direction: an arrival notice for content the surface appends while leaving the operator's scroll alone (the node-detail message list's settle lane, `消息已更新`) describes a row that exists but may be out of view, so its toast is load-bearing and stays — retiring it takes an in-place 「新增 N 条」 control the operator activates, never a scroll the renderer performs.

设置 opens a centered dialog (`#project-settings-backdrop` / `#project-settings-dialog`, reusing the confirm-dialog skin like the exec-whitelist dialog) whose body is one row — 修改密码, then 配置, then the 记住密码自动解锁 checkbox, sharing one center line — plus a footer holding 锁定 at the left and 退出 at the right, and a `✕` (`icon-btn dialog-close-btn`) in the top-right corner instead of a 关闭 button. No row headings and no explanatory copy: the controls are self-describing. 修改密码 opens a second centered dialog (`#password-change-backdrop`) that owns the three password inputs and the 确认修改 button; closing the settings dialog closes it too, and either close path clears the three inputs. Escape closes the innermost dialog first. Both 记住密码自动解锁 checkboxes (this dialog and the boot screen) use the shared `.ui-checkbox-row` / `.ui-checkbox-input` / `.ui-checkbox-box` triple: the native input is invisible and the box plus its `::after` check mark carry the state, so no lucide icon is required. The boot screen in `locked` mode shows only the 请输入密码 field plus that checkbox, whose state comes from `status.auto_unlock`; the explanatory subtitle is hidden there and the `setup` flow keeps its confirm-password and legacy-reset controls. Endpoint paths, credential storage and the lock-vs-exit boundary are owned by `config-and-models.md`「Deployment Unlock Contract」.

配置 is the third dialog layer (`#config-bundle-backdrop`, same skin and the same `✕`), and it is the whole 配置包 surface: a 导出范围 group (配置 / 记忆) plus the 使用项目解锁密码 checkbox over 导出配置包, then the 配置包文件 group, a matching 导入范围 group, 包口令, 导入配置包. Each group governs one direction only: the export pair chooses what goes into the file, the import pair chooses what lands on this machine, and both pairs default to 配置 checked / 记忆 unchecked. A selection with nothing checked is refused client-side before the request and server-side as `bundle_scope_empty` rather than silently falling back to config-only. It carries exactly two prose lines, both risk prompts and nothing else: the export side names what each面 contains and warns that the password is its only defence, the import side states that import replaces the selected面 wholesale, keeps the unlock password (the confirm step asks for it) and needs a restart. Its contract points:

- The two risk paragraphs must be readable end to end without scrolling. The shared `.confirm-text` caps itself at `min(180px, 24vh)` with `overflow-y: auto`, and `.project-settings-body` capped at 420px — together that put the import warning below the fold at any normal window size. This dialog lifts both: `max-height: none` on its paragraphs, and a `min(86vh, 920px)` body so only a genuinely short window falls back to scrolling. Measured at 1440×900 the body needs 505px of 774px available and neither paragraph clips; the paragraph cap only bites below ~533px of viewport height, which is why it is removed rather than raised.
- The checkbox picks between two password lanes shown by `hidden` on the `.resource-field` wrappers, never by removing them: checked leaves the export block with no input at all, unchecked expands 导出口令 + 确认导出口令. `syncConfigBundlePasswordField()` runs on open, on checkbox change, and on close — close also restores the checked default, so the dialog never reopens into the lane the operator last abandoned. The four 范围 checkboxes are read once per submit (`configBundleExportScopes()` / `configBundleImportScopes()`) and reset by the same close path (`resetBundleScopeCheckboxes()`), because an import that was run 记忆-only must not silently stay 记忆-only on the next open. Reading a missing element yields that same default (配置 on, 记忆 off), so the scope state never depends on the DOM having been rendered.
- The native `<input type="file">` is `hidden` and never styled; 选择配置包 proxies a click to it, and `.config-bundle-file-name` echoes the picked file name as the state line. Clearing the input's value clears `files`, so the close path resets both the name and the selection.
- Export never streams bytes through the JSON lane: the POST returns metadata only (filename, entry list, size, the selected `scopes` and `config_entry_count` / `memory_entry_count` — and deliberately not the server-side path), and the UI then follows `ApiClient.getConfigBundleDownloadUrl()` down the shared download lane (an anchor carrying `download`, 见「In-App Media Viewer And Download Lane」) to hit the download route. In the desktop shell that hop only lands if the host allows downloads（闸门与判读见 `operations-and-maintenance.md`「首选一键启动脚本」）. That route accepts only the bundle filename pattern, so the UI cannot ask the server for an arbitrary file under `.g3ku/`. The two per-face counts are the only place the toast gets 共 N 个文件（配置 a、记忆 b） from; the export toast never re-reads the checkboxes, which the close path has already reset.
- Import posts `FormData` (file + 包口令 + `confirm_running_work` + `current_password` + `include_config` + `include_memory`) and asks `/api/bootstrap/exit-check` first, reusing the exit flow's running-work snapshot: with work in flight the confirm dialog requires the 暂停 checkbox before submitting, and a locked process that cannot answer the check is treated as having none. The confirm text names the selected面, so the operator reads 整体替换当前记忆 rather than being told about a config面 they did not select. The same confirm dialog carries the shared optional 口令 row (`#confirm-input-row`, rendered only when `openConfirm` receives an `input` payload) and refuses to submit while it is blank — that password proves the operator holds this machine's credential, and with the config面 selected it is also what the imported master key gets re-wrapped under. A refusal never touches a file, so a blank or wrong 口令 leaves this machine exactly as it was.
- `#confirm-backdrop` sits earlier in the DOM than the settings and 配置 dialogs, so anything that needs `openConfirm()` must close its parent layers first or the confirm renders underneath them. Import does exactly that before opening the confirm.
- A successful import reloads the page: whichever面 was selected changed underneath a live UI, so keeping the old DOM would render against stale state. Its toast prefixes the result with `包来自 X，本机 Y` only when the bundle's `source_app_version` differs from `local_app_version` — same-version ends are the case where the report is about behavior, not about the build.

One collapse system exists in the page chrome:

| System | Controls | State source | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CEO session list | `#ceo-session-panel-toggle` | `S.ceoSessionPanelExpanded`, `.ceo-shell.is-session-panel-expanded`, `data-panel-state` | CEO page column only |

- The top navigation bar has no icon-only shape: the brand text, every nav label and the 设置 label always render, there is no control that hides them, and no stored preference for it. `.sidebar.is-collapsed` is therefore not a live selector — a rule written against it can never match, so restyling the bar means editing the one expanded shape.
- Nav labels are short (`会话 / 任务 / Skill / Tool / 记忆 / 模型 / 外部 / 日志`); `data-view` values are unchanged and each view's own page title keeps its full name. The bar is 56px tall in both themes and at every viewport the layout targets.
- Switching the theme and toggling the CEO session panel are independent, and neither re-renders session, task or resource data nor calls an API.

### CEO Reading Column

- `.ceo-shell` is a two-column grid whose first column is `var(--ui-ceo-session-width)`: 92px collapsed, 288px expanded. A hardcoded `288px` column or `auto` both break the session-panel toggle and are not valid.
- Feed, message bubbles and status notices fill the chat column edge to edge; the chat page carries no centered reading width, so it blends into the page background instead of floating a narrow column inside wide gutters. The composer is the exception on both counts: `.chat-input-area` is `width: min(50vw, 100%)` and centers by `margin-inline: auto`, and it floats over the feed instead of reserving a row of it. The width is anchored to the viewport, not to the chat column, because the column itself narrows when the session panel expands — a percentage-of-column composer visibly shrinks every time the operator opens the session list. Inline padding is zero so the box reads as exactly that width.
- The floating toolbar paints nothing behind itself: `.chat-input-area` is transparent, with no band and no top divider, so the transcript keeps running underneath. Its controls are one size (40px tall, `--ui-radius-control`) but they do not sit beside the textarea: the context-usage brain and attach buttons overlay the textarea's bottom-left corner and send/pause overlays its bottom-right, so `.chat-input-area` is a single-column grid and `.ceo-input-row` is the controls' positioning context. Their insets are 28px inline (8px gutter plus half a 40px control — both sides carry the same value or the band reads as skewed) and 8px from the bottom. The textarea's bottom padding (52px = 40px control + 8px inset + 4px gap) is what keeps typed text out from under them, and its `min-height` (120px) is the resting height the drag floor reuses. `#ceo-send-btn` therefore lives inside `.ceo-input-row` in the DOM, not as a second toolbar column; the legacy layer keeps it as a third grid column of the same row so dropping the active layer still renders the old side-by-side toolbar. A new composer control joins at 40px inside that bottom band rather than introducing another radius. Below 768px the toolbar returns to full width with the page's inline gutter.
- Because the toolbar is one column, the textarea's center line, the top resize band and the 回到最新 button (absolutely positioned over the toolbar) are the same axis; anything that reintroduces a second toolbar column puts those three back out of alignment.
- Out-of-flow means the feed's bottom clearance is not automatic: `watchCeoComposerInset` measures `.chat-input-area` and writes `--ceo-composer-inset` onto `.chat-wrapper`, and `.chat-feed` spends it as bottom padding (the CSS fallback 96px only covers the resting toolbar). A constant there is the bug shape to avoid — the toolbar grows with typing, with a drag, and with the queue / upload / edit lanes stacked above it, and each of those would otherwise park the newest message permanently under the floating box.
- The textarea's top edge doubles as its resize grip: `#ceo-input-resize-handle` is an 8px band whose `#39c5bb` pill sits centered on it. The band is grid-placed into the textarea's own cell, which makes that cell its containing block, so the grab area matches the textarea exactly at every width instead of relying on a hand-computed inset. Move and release are listened for on `window`, not on the band: an 8px target loses the pointer as soon as the drag starts. A drag writes an explicit height that outranks content autosizing (`syncCeoInputHeight` returns early once one exists, longer text then scrolls inside the box), lifted `max-height` included, and the drag range is bounded by the textarea's CSS `min-height` (its 120px resting height) at the bottom and 60% of `.chat-wrapper` at the top so the feed can never be squeezed out.
- The edit-resend banner's container paints nothing, following the context-load notice rule: it is a positioning wrapper and the visible surface is the `.ceo-edit-resend-chip` inside it, whose radius is `--ui-radius-control` so it reads as one family with `.app-toast`. Giving the container its own fill and border draws a second, squarer box behind the chip's rounded corners.
- The expanded session-panel header keeps one center line: `.ceo-session-tabs` is 40px like the 多选 button beside it, but the tabs inside must not carry their own height. The capsule's 1px border plus 4px padding leaves a 30px content box, and the legacy 36px tab starts at that box's top edge and spills 6px below it, which drops 本地/渠道 3px under 多选 and off the center of the `::before` highlight pill. `height: auto` lets the grid row stretch, so the tab box and the highlight pill are always the same box.
- 回合气泡（`.ceo-turn-message .msg-content`）**无条件吃满整列**（`width: 100%`），没有"按内容收缩"的例外：占位态有三条让位规则都曾是按内容收缩的：`.ceo-turn-loading-only .msg-content`（`width: fit-content; max-width: none`）、`.ceo-turn-loading-only .ceo-turn-content`（`width: auto`，三条类选择器、特异性比整列宽那条更高，只改前一条无效）、以及曾用过的 `.msg-content:has(> .assistant-text-loading)`（`width: fit-content`），而 `fit-content` 取的是最宽子项——展开阶段卡时那个子项就是某一轮的工具条横排（`.task-trace-round-strip` 是 `flex-wrap: nowrap`，内在宽度 = 各 chip 相加，实测 1 条 123px / 12 条 1489px），于是气泡宽窄随"那一轮并行了几个工具"变化（实测占位态 1 chip = 203px、12 chip = 1200px），读起来就是"有时变很宽有时不变"。两条例外都取整列宽，条带超出可用宽时自己横向滚（`stripBox` 停在父宽、`scrollWidth` 更大）。**模型回复不装盒**：`.message.system .msg-content` 去底、去描边、去圆角、去内缩并给到整列宽（`.message.system .msg-content:not(.ceo-internal-ack-content)` 是同档的另一条规则，保住内部轮状态条的虚线盒），正文因此直接印在会话面上，左右只剩 feed 自己的 12px 内缩；内部轮状态条靠那个 `:not()` 保住自己的虚线盒，用户气泡与轨道容器不受影响。没有轨道的系统兜底气泡同样吃满整列（`.message.system .message-stack { width: 100% }`），只有用户车道仍按 `fit-content` 收拢。Finalizing a turn clears `assistant-text-loading` and its `role="status"` label.
- The placeholder's spinner (`.interaction-step-icon.is-spinning svg`, the same class the running tool-step icons use) is exempt from the stylesheet's global `prefers-reduced-motion` animation freeze, alongside the compression arc and the token-growth glow: while a model turn is in flight the rotation is the only liveness cue, and on a machine with OS animations off a frozen glyph reads as a session that stopped responding. The exemption is a more-specific selector with `!important` inside a reduced-motion block, and the keyframe runs 0→360deg `linear infinite`, so the wrap is seamless. 工具行的转圈用 lucide `loader`（十二辐条），占位指示器仍是 `loader-circle`（缺弧圆环）。
- No status glyph wears a ring. `.interaction-step-icon` — shared by the stage-trace tool steps and the in-bubble loading placeholder — is a bare 26×26 box with no `background`, `border` or `radius`; state rides on `color` plus the always-present title text. The 26×26 box stays so the icon column's baseline does not move. The placeholder's arc is 16px against the track's 14px, and that override must repeat `interaction-step-icon` in its selector to beat the same-specificity `.interaction-step-icon svg` rule. The compression divider's `::before` paints only `border-top-color` and leaves the other three sides transparent, so it is one rotating arc rather than a ring on a track: the paused state drops the pseudo-element entirely (`.is-bars-only` → `content: none`), which makes that arc the only visual difference between 压缩中 and 已暂停, and a static full-circle track would erase it.
- Below 768px an expanded session panel leaves the grid flow: `.ceo-shell` drops to a single column so the chat keeps the full width, and the panel overlays it at `min(288px, calc(100vw - 24px))`. Keeping two columns there lets grid auto-placement drop the chat wrapper into the 92px track.

### Status Color Semantics

`data-status` keeps one meaning across every page, and the label text is always present so color is never the only signal:

- `running` / `in_progress` → `--ui-info` blue; `inspecting` → `--ui-inspecting` purple. Execution and acceptance are both "in progress" but read as two lifecycle tiers, so a running node, its task-detail pill and its task-hall card carry the same blue, while an acceptance node stays distinguishable at a glance. Brand teal is not a lifecycle color at any tier — it stays reserved for selection and primary actions.
- `completed` / `passed` / `success` → success green; `failed` → danger red; `unpassed` → warning amber.
- `paused` / `queued` / `pending` / `waiting` / `blocked` / `unknown`, plus read-only and disabled → neutral gray.
- An unmapped `data-status` renders neutral gray with its original text; it never inherits brand or danger styling and never renders unstyled.
- Every `.status-badge` box uses `--ui-radius-control`, the same radius as buttons, so a badge sitting in a button row reads as one family; the badge's own height comes from the surface it is on (24px tags in tree nodes, cards and drawers; the task-detail pill lifts to the 40px header control tier). Lifecycle color rules own `background` / `color` only; the base badge box owns the border (`1px` of 50% `currentColor`, i.e. 50% of that rule's own text color), so a surface rule may resize a badge without repainting it and a new lifecycle tier contributes a fill plus a text color and nothing else. The neutral tier is the one exception: it re-asserts the hueless `--ui-border`, because 50% of its gray text ink would paint a heavier ring than the badge needs.
- Node cards take the solid tint tier — `--ui-*-soft` fill with a 50%-alpha `--ui-*` border and the normal text color — instead of the legacy `color-mix(soft 24%, panel)`, which diluted the tier a second time and read as no fill at all on a white page. Running and inspecting share the brand teal container (teal is the "in progress" surface) while their badges split blue and purple; the running card's breathing `::after` ring stays at the legacy 88% because it is the in-flight attention cue, not a container edge. Two traps: `.execution-tree-item` is the `<li>` wrapping a whole subtree and only carries the connector color, so painting it turns a branch into one large block and destroys the card edges; and the badge inside a card keeps a surface fill, because card and badge would otherwise share one token and only the border would separate them. `.selected` re-asserts the brand border after the status rules, otherwise selecting a colored card leaves only the shadow as the cue. The user turn bubble is a member of the same tier (`--ui-brand-soft` fill, 50%-alpha brand border): the chat column and the execution tree are read side by side, and a full-opacity brand border on the bubble paints it a tier brighter than the running nodes it sits next to.

Task hall status badges are labeled in Chinese from `taskStatusLabel` (`运行 / 成功 / 失败 / 暂停 / 未通过 / 未知`); the task detail pill uses its own Chinese mapping. Both must stay Chinese so the two surfaces agree.

### Page Surfaces Are Flat

Admin pages draw one level of boxes only. The page-level containers (`.resource-list-panel`, `.resource-detail-panel`, `.memory-column`, `.audit-feed-panel`) are transparent and borderless, so repeated content — task cards, Skill/Tool cards, model rows, memory records, token rows — is the only framed layer. Drawers and dialogs keep surface fill, border and `--ui-shadow-dialog` because they float above the page rather than nesting inside it. A new admin surface must not wrap content in a second bordered panel.

Every view keeps its own `<h1>` in the DOM — it is the page's accessible name, so do not delete it. On the resource views other than `#view-models` and `#view-external` the title is visually hidden (clipped to 1px, kept in the accessibility tree) because the top bar already marks the current section, and the header row carries only toolbar and status content. Hiding the text is not sufficient: the wrapper that held it (`.task-header-main`, `.resource-header-title`) also leaves the flex flow, otherwise it still contributes width and the first header control starts one gap to the right of the page padding. `#view-models` and `#view-external` are the two views whose header owns a visible title: each shows `<h1>` plus one `.subtitle` line, and a new view follows the hidden-title lane unless its page needs that description.

Header controls share one left edge at the page padding, and one 12px gap between siblings. A header whose only left-edge item is 刷新 pins that button there absolutely (Skill, Tool, 记忆, 日志). `#view-models` and `#view-external` keep their whole action group in flow and push it to the right edge with `margin-left: auto`, so 刷新 / 添加配置 / 最大轮数 / 上下文信息更新时间 read as one right-aligned row beside the title. New header controls join the group that matches their page and never re-introduce a per-page offset.

Header and dialog buttons are flat by default: `.toolbar-btn.success` — 保存并连接, 签发桥接 token, and the dialog primaries 确定 / 保存 / 确认修改 / 添加 / 启用模型 — renders exactly like a plain toolbar button (surface fill, `--ui-border`, `--ui-text`, hover `--ui-neutral-soft`); the legacy class painted it with a `--ui-brand` fill, a `color-mix(..., transparent)` border and a same-color glow, which after the light-theme accents were darkened read as a blue-green block. Two boxes stay filled, and both are the single pending-commit action of their page: the model page's dirty-state `保存` (`#view-models #model-roles-save-btn`, `--ui-brand` fill + `--ui-on-brand` text — the id-scoped rule exists because the flat reset also targets that id) and the active navigation item.

The 任务排序 / 全局任务树深度 buttons label their name in `--ui-text` — a gray label next to a black one reads as a disabled control even though both are clickable.

There are two control tiers and no third: **large** = 40px tall, **small** = 32px (`toolbar-btn small`, e.g.「回到最新树」and the 外部 page actions). Both carry `--ui-radius-control` (currently 16px), 13px text, `--ui-bg-surface` fill, 1px `--ui-border`, no shadow. Because the tier radius is 16px, anything 32px tall or shorter in the same family renders as a pill — the 24px `.status-badge` and `.memory-op-chip` boxes are pills by design at this radius, not a regression to "fix". The large tier covers page-header controls (`.toolbar-btn`, `.resource-search`, `.resource-select-trigger`, 排序/树深度 main button), the CEO composer row, the session panel's first row, and the task-detail row — 返回 icon button, 状态 pill, 初始提示词, 活跃节点, 错误日志, Token统计 — where the icon-only button is a 40px square so it never reads as a smaller control than its labelled neighbours. A new control joins one of these two tiers rather than introducing a third height, a legacy radius, or a larger font. Radius tiers are `--ui-radius-control` 16px / `--ui-radius-card` 12px / `--ui-radius-panel` 14px, so controls are currently the roundest family in the UI; raising the control tier further, or wanting cards to read as calmer than buttons, means editing those three values in one place rather than component rules. Chat bubbles (`.message .msg-content`) sit at `--ui-radius-control` as well, with all four corners equal and no tail corner, so a bubble and the composer controls stacked in the same column read as one family; the card radius stays the card family's value and is not a bubble fallback.

Three families deliberately stay outside the tier, so a sweep for "all controls at 40/16/13" must stop at them: **card-shaped controls** (`.resource-list-item`, `.execution-tree-node`, `.memory-card`) keep `--ui-radius-card` / the node's own 18px corners and size to content; **toggle switches** (`.tool-governance-switch`) keep a 999px track because the radius is the switch's shape, not its box; **content disclosure rows** (`.task-trace-summary`, the node drawer's 完整上下文) are headings that open, and their type scale belongs to the transcript, not to the toolbar. Legacy `org_graph.css` still hardcodes 42px/14px on `.compact-resource-header-actions` children and 46px/16px on `.resource-select-trigger`; those are dead values under the active layer — the tests that assert them guard the legacy file, not the rendered page, so measure in the browser before "fixing" them.

The task hall header is one row: 任务排序 → 全局任务树深度 hug the left edge, and the status capsules after them are pushed to the right edge at every width. 多选态里那颗读数条整条让位（`renderTaskPerformanceBar` 在模式下直接早退，不清内容：退出多选先亮上一次读数，再由轮询刷新），否则动作组把它挤到换行，两排按钮读起来像叠在一起。 Selection is one button in that same row: 多选. With it off the row shows nothing else and the cards carry no visible box; clicking it reveals `#task-selection-bar` in the same row — 已选 N 个 → 按状态选 (全部任务 + six status buckets, replacing the set across the whole loaded list) → 暂停 / 开始 / 删除 → 取消选择 — and puts a checkbox on every card. Action buttons enable off the current selection, so a batch run is one click rather than a mode toggle plus a nested menu, and 取消选择 ends the whole operation (clears the set and turns the mode off). There is no second bar above the grid. 重试 is deliberately absent: `runTaskBatchAction` has no retry branch and no batch retry endpoint exists. The pressure state and the CPU/memory/disk reading share one capsule, and each of the three resource numbers colors itself by its own occupancy tier (≥75% warning, ≥90% danger) so the reading is not pinned to one color.「任务排序」and「全局任务树深度」are single borderless buttons carrying only their name and a chevron — the chosen option is not echoed on the button — and their dropdown appears below the button on click. At ≥1200px the three buttons and the capsules share one row; below that the capsules take a full-width row of their own and stay flush right.

A dropdown that opens inside a clipped or scrolling surface is lifted out of it: a `resource-select` carrying `data-resource-select-portal="true"` moves its option panel to `document.body` while open, positions it `fixed` against the trigger's viewport rect (flipping above when the space below is shorter than the panel), and returns it to its shell on close — 模型配置 弹窗的 深度思考 下拉就是这么一个控件。Keeping the panel inside the shell instead adds to the scroll container's overflow and makes the browser scroll the focused option into view, so opening it shifts the whole form. The same escape-by-reparenting shape owns the `配置说明` popover. `enhanceResourceSelects()` drops any portaled panel whose owner shell is gone, which is what a modal rebuild leaves behind.

### Responsive Bands

The task hall grid is content-sized — `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(300px, 1fr))` — so its column count follows the available width, and a long token count wraps inside a fixed-size card rather than the card shrinking to fit one line. Every other page grid may only use the shared bands 1200 / 1024 / 900 / 768 / 640px: Skill/Tool cards step 4 → 3 → 2 → 1 at 1200 / 1024 / 768; model role cards 4 → 2 → 1 at 1200 / 1024; memory keeps two columns at ≥1024px; external access keeps two columns at ≥900px. When space runs short between two bands, tighten gap, card padding and control min-width before dropping a column, and never add a page-specific breakpoint.

## Frontend Vendor Asset Update Contract

`g3ku/web/frontend_assets.py` owns the third-party static assets the console ships with: the Lucide icon bundle and the self-hosted Google Fonts files. Each asset has two state carriers, and they answer different questions.

- **Tracked manifest** (`g3ku/web/frontend/vendor/lucide-manifest.json`, `.../vendor/fonts/google-fonts-manifest.json`) states the identity of what is committed: `installed_version`, `installed_at`, `installed_revision`, plus `bundle` / `stylesheet` / `source_url` / `font_files` / `font_versions`. It changes only together with the vendored bytes, i.e. when a commit actually upgrades the package.
- **Probe overlay** (`<data root>/vendor-updates.json`, one slot per asset: `lucide`, `google-fonts`) carries what a running device learns about the outside world: `latest_version`, `latest_revision`, `latest_font_versions`, `update_available`, `update_mode`, `check_interval_days`, `checked_at`, `last_error`.

`_read_manifest()` returns the merged view (overlay wins) so readers keep working with one dictionary, and `_write_manifest()` splits by field ownership on write, so call sites still pass complete payloads. The rule this encodes is that **a background probe leaves the tracked manifest byte-identical**: `notify` moves only the overlay, and a write is skipped whenever the serialized static fields already match the file on disk, compared after folding CRLF to LF. Rewriting identical bytes is not a no-op on Windows: under `core.autocrlf=true` git checks the file out as CRLF while the writer emits LF, so the tree ends up "same bytes, reported modified", and that reported state is what gates an upgrade (see `operations-and-maintenance.md`「新设备首次安装与升级」). A manifest left in that state stops every later 「重启并更新」 before any code is touched, which is why the comparison sits in the writer rather than relying on the guard.

Replacing bytes goes through `_install_lucide_release()` / `_install_font_payload()`: fetch the npm tarball member or the CSS URLs, write the artifact plus its tracked manifest, and record `update_available = false` in the overlay. `update_mode` comes from `G3KU_FRONTEND_<ASSET>_UPDATE_MODE` / `G3KU_FRONTEND_ASSET_UPDATE_MODE` with values `off` / `notify` / `auto` (default `notify`); `notify` logs the finding and touches nothing but the overlay, `auto` replaces the vendored file. `LUCIDE_DEFAULT_PINNED_VERSION` is the recovery pin used only when the bundle file itself is missing.

## CEO Composer Runtime

The Leader/CEO composer has two distinct runtime behaviors that maintainers need to keep straight.

### 1. Active-Turn Button Semantics

- If the current session is idle and the composer is empty, the primary button stays in a disabled `send` state.
- If a user-visible turn is currently running and the composer is empty, the primary button switches to `pause`.
- If the composer contains text or attachments, the primary button switches back to `send` even when a user turn or heartbeat turn is still running.
- Channel sessions split on `can_message`, not on the read-only flag: a registered `ext:` session behaves like a local one (composer, attach and send all live, and a running turn still offers `pause`), while `china:*` archives and registry-less ext rows keep the button disabled with the read-only label. Even there, a running turn must switch the button to `pause`: the writability constraint is what blocks sending (`client.user_message` → `channel_session_readonly`) and it must never block pausing. The pause request is the same `client.pause_turn` lane as local sessions, and `client.resume_interrupt` (工具审批续跑) is likewise open on channel sessions.
- History actions never follow `can_message`: 编辑重发 / Fork stay hidden on every channel session (the snapshot gate returns no flags for channel keys and the REST endpoints re-validate).

This is intentional. The composer button means "pause only when the user has not prepared a follow-up payload", not "pause whenever a turn is active".

#### 1.1 「还在跑」的判据必须现算，不能读单帧布尔量

暂停入口的可见性（以及编辑重发那颗按钮的点击守卫）判的是**这条会话此刻是否还不在稳定态**，判据在 `org_graph_app.js::activeCeoSessionIsNonStable` 里逐次现算：

- 目录行的 `is_running`（正向证据；它滞后，不能当否定证据）；
- 在飞快照 `inflight_turn.status ∈ {running, in_progress, active}`；
- 待审批中断（快照里的 approval 中断，或 `hasActiveCeoApprovalBlockingState`）；
- 压缩在途（会话级 `compression.status == "running"`，含回合外手动压缩）；
- 本地回合元素仍挂着加载态（刚发出消息、服务端还没回第一帧的兜底）。

`ceoSessionPauseAvailable()` 再把这份现算与 `S.ceoTurnActive` 取并，主按钮与 `requestCeoPause` / `handleCeoPrimaryAction` 都只认它。

两条历史 bug 都由"读单帧布尔量"造成，改动时不要退回去：

- **间歇性消失（按不下暂停）**：心跳内部轮的 `ceo.state`（`status != running`、四项未了结全为零）会把 `S.ceoTurnActive` 写成 `false`，而这一轮其实还在跑（在飞快照仍说 `running`）。按钮于是闪一下就没，用户点不到。
- **永久挂着暂停（会话已停）**：运行时错误把 `status` 落成 `error` 并清掉在飞快照，但没有任何通道会再把那个布尔量置回 `false`；按钮就停在暂停语义上，而按下去服务端只回 `accepted=false`。

`S.ceoTurnActive` 本身**不写回**这份现算结果：它同时兼着"草稿入队还是直发"的判据（见下面 §2 与 `applyCeoState` 的注释），改写它会把派发闸门一起带歪。同理 `.ceo-turn-active` 仍由它驱动，暂停/出错之后它自然变假，所以那颗编辑按钮不需要在 CSS 里额外豁免。

### 2. Queued Follow-Ups

- The composer-side queue lane above the input box is two lists merged on render: the runtime's accepted queue (read from `ceo.state.queued_follow_up_messages`, which carries `content` / `attachments` / `metadata._transcript_turn_id`) painted first, then the browser's not-yet-sent drafts. 行上不写状态文字：条目还挂在输入区上面就代表没发出去，再印一个状态词是同一件事说第二遍。三态只差在动作集上——本地草稿给 插话（仅回合在跑时）、编辑、删除三颗；服务端排队里的条目给 编辑、删除两颗（它已在 runtime 的队列里等下一轮，插无可插）；已转发而状态帧还没跟上的那一小段一颗都不给（runtime 已持有，既没有可重武装的对象也没有可撤的行，等认出它已被本轮代表的那一帧把它摘掉，判据见下面退场记账那条）。插话 = 只把这一条按普通 `client.user_message` 车道转出去；编辑 (pencil) 把条目弄走并把正文与附件还给输入框（走草稿车道，不自动发送）；删除 (trash-2) 同样弄走但不还正文。编辑与删除对服务端条目都先打 `POST /api/ceo/sessions/{id}/queued-follow-ups/withdraw` `{turn_id}`——那边的队列才是真相，成功才动本地——两条走同一个 `releaseCeoQueuedFollowUp`，差别只在 `refillComposer`。The endpoint answers `409 follow_up_not_queued` for a turn the runtime already consumed, because deleting that row would erase an answered user message, and it emits a `state_snapshot` afterwards so other tabs painting the same session converge. 浏览器把这一颗 409 当**退场信号**而不是错误：按"服务端已不持有这条"摘掉条目并给 info 提示（"已被本轮接走"），不给 failure toast——409 在这里说的是"你看到的行是过期的"，让用户以为删除失败是把一个正常交接读成故障。
- 队列被接走的那一刻，服务端必须自己广播一帧 state，两个消费点都要：回合尾的 drain（`websocket_ceo.py` 的回合链在 drain 之后、归档转场之前）与同一可见轮内 `call_model` 之前的安全边界（`_consume_session_follow_up_messages_before_call_model` 接走之后）。候选条来自 state 帧的整表替换，所以退场只能由 state 帧驱动：`ceo.reply.final.user_messages` 担不了这个判据——一个还在跑的续跑轮不会发 final，于是一条已被接走的补充会一直挂在输入区上方、按钮点得动，而服务端早就不持有它。排查"补充消息不消失 / 撤不下"先查接走之后有没有 state 帧落地，再查浏览器有没有按 409 退场。
- 「这一回合还在跑」是**草稿入队还是直接发出去的唯一判据**，所以它不能只看一帧 `status` / `is_running`：`applyCeoState` 按帧到达即写会把两次模型调用之间的空档读成"没回合在跑"，一条草稿就会被整批直接发出去。判据改成"帧里确实没有未了结的活"——在飞工具、待审批批次、服务端排队条目、压缩在途四项都为零，且没有未定型的在飞轮，才放行 `false`。这一判据的输入全部来自同一帧，不引入浏览器侧计时器。
- 正文一行封顶（`nowrap` + ellipsis）：补充消息可以是整篇正文，不封顶会把输入区上方撑成一块盖住会话的面板。展开入口是那颗 chevron，而**它只在量出溢出时才画**：CSS 判不出"这一行放得下"，所以每次渲染后按行量 `scrollWidth > clientWidth`（`measureCeoFollowUpSingleLine`，容器尺寸变化由 ResizeObserver 再量一次）。三条判读约束是这条车道的合同本身——宽度为 0（面板隐藏或还没排版）时不下结论，宁可留着入口；展开态的正文盒是换行排版的，量出来永远"放得下"，所以展开着的行不参与测量（否则点开即塌）；结论按条目 id 记在模块级集合里，队列每帧重画，DOM 上的 `hidden` 活不过一次 render。三颗动作与 chevron 都带 `title`：图标按钮没有可见文案，功能只能在悬停时说。
- Browser storage (`sessionStorage`) is only ever the authority for drafts that have not been handed to the runtime. The runtime-held half must survive a refresh, a different tab, and a process restart, so a re-opened session shows it from the state snapshot rather than losing it with the local cache. The bound this sets: an item still held by the browser is lost if that tab closes before the running turn ends.
- Sending while a turn is active does not interrupt the current turn and does not reach the backend at all: the item stays in the browser lane and starts its own fresh user turn, in arrival order, once the running turn closes (`maybeDispatchQueuedCeoFollowUps` on the final frame and on any state frame that finds the session idle). A chip therefore shows 插话 only while a turn is running; that affordance forwards just that one item on the ordinary `client.user_message` lane, which is what merges it into the running turn's next safe boundary. A message arriving while a manual context compression is in flight takes the same merge path (inbound-hold and dispatch contract: `runtime-overview.md`「压缩窗口：入站闸门与基线写入仲裁」).
- The backend-owned follow-up queue is authoritative once that send succeeds, and chips already handed off to the runtime must never be re-sent when the turn later closes — the browser's own idle dispatcher only forwards items with no `runtime_sent_at` marker, which is what keeps the server-side dispatcher and the client-side one from duplicating a message.
- CEO/frontdoor consumes queued follow-ups at the safe boundary right before the next `call_model` send of the same visible turn. The runtime appends them as independent `user` messages to the current request body instead of concatenating them into one synthetic supplement string.
- If the current visible turn finishes before another `call_model` round happens, the backend immediately starts the next fresh user turn from the queued follow-ups after the current turn closes.
- Each queued item still remains its own user message in transcript persistence; batching only changes which next LLM request sees that group first.
- A single visible turn can hold multiple distinct user messages once follow-ups are consumed. The browser treats two user messages as equivalent only when their turn id (when present on both), content, and attachments all match; matching on turn id alone drops a distinct follow-up and leaves the user's supplement invisible.
- Once a queued follow-up has been handed off to the backend, it should still remain in the composer-side queue lane until runtime snapshot/final-reply data can prove that the follow-up has been consumed into a visible turn. 那条证明按 **turn_id** 记账：服务端条目 id 是 `server:<_transcript_turn_id>`，而在飞帧/收尾帧带去的用户消息带同一个 `turn_id`，同号即认定已接走，并把该 id 记进本页面生命周期内不撤的账（`retireCeoTakenFollowUpsFromRepresentedMessages`），因此**迟到的 `ceo.state` 读数不得把它画回来**——state 帧是整表替换的镜像来源，只靠它自己摘不掉一份晚到的旧表。这一账只管镜像那一半；本地草稿与在飞条目的等价判据仍是 turn_id + 正文 + 附件三者（下一条）。
- Browser rendering must not create a provisional transcript bubble merely because a follow-up was accepted by the backend queue. Queue acceptance and visible conversation placement are intentionally different stages.
- `ceo.reply.final` may include `user_messages` for the just-finished visible turn. This is the authoritative current-turn user batch and exists specifically so the frontend can decide whether runtime-sent follow-ups belonged to that same reply or to a later chained turn.
- If `ceo.reply.final.user_messages` includes a runtime-sent follow-up, the browser must rebuild the final visible order as `current turn user batch -> final assistant reply` and clear the matched queue entry.
- If a runtime-sent follow-up does not appear in the just-finished turn's `user_messages`, the browser should keep it in the queue lane until a later fresh user turn or transcript snapshot represents it authoritatively.
- `snapshot.ceo.messages` must also avoid replaying running-turn `pending` user transcript rows as ordinary history bubbles. During a live running turn, authoritative current-turn user placement comes from `inflight_turn.user_messages`, not from flat transcript replay.
- When a running follow-up is actually consumed into the next model send of the same visible conversation lane, the runtime also archives the pre-follow-up assistant execution bubble into visible UI history before the consumed user bubble is restored. That archive is UI-visible but prompt-hidden, so refresh/reconnect can preserve the same visual ordering without polluting later prompt history.
- The archive turn id carries a `:followup:` segment. When the browser renders an archived turn with that segment, it marks the last group in that archive as the interrupted group (interruption marker on its title), because a follow-up consumed mid-flight splits that rail between the archive (tools already done) and the continuation turn (remaining tools).
- A consumed follow-up's user bubble is inserted immediately before the current live turn element, not appended to the end of the feed, so it reads in chronological order between the archive and the continuation.
- The same ordering rule governs the snapshot restore lane: `inflight_turn` / `preserved_turn` carry the *current batch*, which is not the same thing as "just sent". `restoreCeoInflightTurn` therefore lands each user bubble at its own `timestamp` — rendered bubbles expose it as `dataset.ceoTimestamp`, written by `addMsg` — and appends only when no parseable timestamp exists (the live typing path). Batching a stale message into the current turn would otherwise surface it below the newest reply and read as the user re-asking an old question; the authoritative queue-side fix is `runtime-overview.md`「压缩窗口：入站闸门与基线写入仲裁」, this is the display-layer bound.

### Model Retry Visibility UI Contract

- `ConfigChatBackend` publishes live-only `model_retry_status` before each real retry (per-model backoff round or same-model key-switch resend) and clears the field when retrying exits. A cross-model fallback is normal chain operation: it is counted but emits no status. The status carries `state=retrying`, `retry_count` equal to provider requests actually sent so far (hence the ordinal of the retry about to happen), the model retry round, the model refs, an `error_message` capped at 4096 chars to keep websocket payloads bounded, the backoff delay (0 for key-switch emissions), and local-offset `last_retry_at` / `next_retry_at` timestamps. The task-node frame sanitizer (`_sanitize_model_retry_status`) is a field whitelist — the timestamps must be listed there or they are stripped — and its error-text bound equals the emit-side limit because the browser expands the toast to the full received text. The CEO snapshot deep-copies the whole status and needs no whitelist entry.
- 同一字段还有第二个 live 取值 `state=waiting_upstream`：这一跳的流还在收分片、却连续 60 秒没有任何载荷分片（正文 / 思考 / 工具参数）可给界面。它带的读数只有 `waiting_seconds`、`chunk_count`、`chunk_kinds`（`delay_seconds` 恒 0，且**不带** `error_message` 与重试时刻——它不是重试，没有任何东西失败过），收尾同样发 `state=cleared`。判别值只有三处字面量的家：`g3ku/providers/fallback.py` 的 `MODEL_RETRY_STATE_*` / `MODEL_RETRY_LIVE_STATES`（Python 判读点全部读它）与前端两份 live-state 集合；新增取值必须同时改这两处与节点侧白名单，否则状态会在一处重建层被静默判成 `None`。
- For CEO sessions, running `inflight_turn.model_retry_status` reaches the browser through the `snapshot.ceo` / `ceo.turn.patch` lanes. The browser shows a warning toast above the conversation feed while the state is live: `retrying` renders `模型自动重试中 · 第 N 次重试 · 最新 HH:MM:SS · 下次 HH:MM:SS · <error>`（clocks render only when present）, `waiting_upstream` renders `等待上游响应 N 秒 · 已收 M 分片`（一个概念一个带标签的整数，不谎报重试次数）; the toast disappears when a snapshot clears the field or the session leaves the running state. Long error text is visually clamped to two lines with an expand affordance (click or Enter/Space toggles the full text).
- For task nodes, the runtime frame exposes the same field through `task.live.patch`. Node detail reads the selected frame and shows the same toast below its header, with the same two copy shapes. Live patches update the toast while the detail drawer is open, and opening the drawer during a retry or an upstream wait restores it from the current frame.
- The status is transient observability state, not transcript history. It is not appended to persisted CEO messages, does not backfill historical node output, and is cleared on success, terminal failure, pause, discard, or config-refresh-based turn rebuild. A failed websocket delivery is covered by the next low-frequency snapshot/patch or by reopening the session/node while the runtime is still retrying.
- 状态字段有**两条并行投影车道，字段集必须一致**：worker 侧 `TaskLogService._public_runtime_frame` 直接展开帧 payload，天然带 `await_marker` / `await_started_at` / `model_retry_status`；web 侧快照投影 `TaskLiveFrame`（`main/monitoring/models.py`，由 `query_service._projection_live_state` 构造）逐字段列举，漏一个就会在每次树快照落地时被抹掉——前端把帧索引整体重建，快照缺项等价于该字段被清空。重试 toast 在分发期间长期不显示即为这一类：分发会反复重拉树快照。新增帧字段时同时改两处，并按 `tests/resources/test_distribution_frozen_ledger.py` 的字段平价断言补一条。
- 节点被子树屏障冻结期间，重试态写在批次帧缓冲里、随"冻结不丢在飞工具回合"的既有合同一起延迟落盘，因此这一窗口内 toast 可以合法地不出现。合同见 `main-task-runtime.md`「分发状态机与屏障」。
- If the chat is stuck but no retry toast appears, check that the actual failing boundary is the retryable `ConfigChatBackend` path rather than an outer idle/preflight/tool wait, then inspect the `on_model_retry_status` callback, `inflight_turn.model_retry_status`, and the node runtime frame field in that order; for a task node, additionally compare the two lanes' payloads (`GET` 树快照 vs 首帧 `task.live.patch`) to rule out the field-parity failure above. 回合"界面冻住而连接没断"有两种长相，读的是同一字段：`state=retrying` 是失败后退避中（带 provider 错误与下次重试时刻），`state=waiting_upstream` 是没失败、只是上游这一跳一直不给载荷分片（带秒数与分片数）；后者还能在日志里按 `no payload chunk for` 数发生次数。

### 2.2. Regulatory Approval Flow

- Web CEO has a second blocking composer lane besides ordinary running turns: a pending `frontdoor_tool_approval_batch`.
- The websocket/runtime path emits that batch through `ceo.turn.interrupt`, and reconnect/session-restore should also be able to rebuild it from `GET /api/ceo/sessions/{session_id}/pending-interrupts` or the paused snapshot lane.
- The frontend review UX is intentionally split:
  - the operator reviews one risky tool call at a time in a toast-like approval card,
  - but the browser must not resume the agent per item.
- The authoritative resume payload is one batch submission:
  - `client.resume_interrupt`
  - `resume.type="submit_batch_review"`
  - `resume.batch_id`
  - `resume.decisions=[{tool_call_id, decision, note?}, ...]`
- The browser may let the operator move backward, change earlier choices, and keep a session-local draft, but it must submit a complete decision for every `review_item` in the batch before the backend resumes.
- Rejection notes are optional operator text, but they only belong to rejected items. The frontend should not send a `note` field for approved items.
- Clicking the parameter preview opens a separate full-args modal. Long argument bodies must stay scrollable, and clicking outside that modal should close it.
- While a regulatory approval batch is pending, the composer should behave as blocked-active state rather than as ordinary paused state:
  - no new user message send,
  - no queued follow-up dispatch,
- session/page switching is allowed while the batch is pending, but the pending review must remain session-local draft state and reappear when the operator returns to that CEO session.
- Frontend pause rendering must not rely only on `source="approval"`. Approval pauses may arrive with ordinary `source="user"` plus approval interrupts in the inflight/paused snapshot lane; in that case the browser should keep the current visible turn in the approval-waiting state instead of finalizing a new `已暂停` history bubble.
- Once the operator submits the batch, the browser should clear the local draft for that batch and wait for the normal runtime events (`ceo.state`, `ceo.reply.final`, etc.) to continue the conversation.

### 2.3. Streamed Reply Delta Contract

- `/ws/ceo` remains the single duplex transport for Web CEO. Browser sends, pause/resume control, tool events, and assistant reply delivery still share the same websocket connection; streamed assistant text is not a separate SSE channel.
- User-visible assistant text may arrive on a dedicated live-only event `ceo.reply.delta`.
- The payload is intentionally small:
  - `turn_id`
  - `source`
  - `text`
  - `seq`
- `text` is the authoritative assistant text of the stream's latest thinking segment: the previous segment stays visible until the next model-call boundary resets it, so the live block shows only the latest segment rather than a whole-turn accumulation; it is neither a raw provider token nor a stage-trace snapshot.
- The browser should treat `ceo.reply.delta` as a cheap text-only lane:
  - render the latest-segment residual into the turn's own bubble (`.assistant-text`, plain text while the turn runs). The turn rail never carries streamed text: a running turn's output has to be readable outside the stage cards from the first token, not only after closeout. `ceo.reply.final` writes the authoritative markdown into that same element.
  - the bubble belongs to this lane only while it carries `.pending` — the marker that says the content is live-lane content. A bubble holding authoritative text (finalized markdown, a silent-turn body, `已暂停`) is never overwritten, not even when the residual goes empty (the empty-residual fallback to the loading spinner is gated on the same marker).
  - update cached `inflight_turn.assistant_text` for reconnect/session restore
  - never treat the delta as durable transcript history and never rerender the turn rail from it
  - this lane writes once per animation frame, so nothing in it may cost a document-wide pass: rewriting the loading markup re-arms `<i data-lucide>` and pulls `lucide.createIcons()` (a whole-document scan) into every frame. `icons()` runs only on the frame that creates the bubble; the loading state is only rewritten when its class is actually absent. The frame budget spent here is what decides whether the operator can scroll while the model is talking.
- Streamed text renders through a plain-text path while the turn runs; `ceo.reply.final` remains the authoritative closeout, where full markdown render, transcript finalization, canonical-context finalization, and visible-turn completion happen.
- The CEO turn body renders as the turn timeline, not as a single overwritten bubble; see "CEO Turn Timeline Rendering Contract" for the layout and field rules.
- Assistant transcript entries carry the turn's `turns[]` (narration + tool records) so the rail survives reloads; the session-level ledger is never copied into each row.
- `ceo.turn.patch` is not the high-frequency assistant-text streaming lane; it is the lower-frequency lane for inflight/preserved snapshot refreshes, state transitions, reconnect bootstrap, tool/interrupt-related snapshot changes, and stage-state progression: the frontdoor graph emits `frontdoor_stage_synced` after each graph-node boundary sync of the session-stage/canonical state, and the websocket relay answers with a live turn patch, so a newly submitted stage and its first tool rounds reach the browser without waiting for the next tool event.
- That lane is rate-limited per connection by `_CEO_TURN_PATCH_MIN_INTERVAL_S`: building one patch walks the session's whole accumulated turns ledger (measured 100-270 ms of event-loop CPU on a long channel session), so a tool-event burst would otherwise spend the turn rebuilding frames and the feed would only catch up at the end. Inside the window at most one trailing patch is scheduled, and it is rebuilt from **current** runtime state at flush time rather than from the triggering event, so a dropped intermediate frame loses nothing (its delta is always relative to the last persisted assistant row) and a turn that already ended flushes as cleared state. Turn-terminating frames (`ceo.reply.final`, `ceo.internal.ack`, `ceo.error`) force the pending patch out first, which keeps patch-before-final ordering for fast turns instead of losing their rail, and keeps a stale `running`/`paused` patch from landing after the closeout and being re-opened as a ghost turn row.

### 2.4. Runtime Error Contract

- `/ws/ceo` may also emit `ceo.error` when a visible turn fails before `ceo.reply.final`.
- The frontend should treat `ceo.error.data.message` as the authoritative operator-facing text for that failure boundary, not as optional debug metadata.
- Backend error delivery has an explicit empty-message fallback. If the raw exception string is empty (for example a bare `MemoryError`), websocket delivery must reuse the session snapshot `last_error.message` when available, and otherwise emit a non-empty fallback message instead of leaving the browser to show `unknown error`.
- This matters especially for memory-pressure failures during request-artifact persistence: the operator-visible contract is “a readable runtime failure message plus a terminal error state”, not “blank message that the browser turns into `unknown error`”.

### 2.5. Image Upload Gating

- Whether uploads become provider-visible image content is judged **per model — the model this send lands on**. At assembly time that is the chain head; when the chain later advances to another member, the throat point in `main/runtime/chat_backend.py` applies the same judgment to that target and rewrites the blocks in place. The judgment is written once (`g3ku/config/model_manager.image_multimodal_target_decision`, answering capability plus display name) and shared by the frontdoor, the node loop and the throat point; the field itself is owned by `config-and-models.md`「Image Multimodal Binding Flag」. When the judged model cannot take images the send does **not** fail and the chain does **not** advance because of a picture: each image turns into one text line carrying its absolute path and the name of the model config that lacks the flag, so the model can fetch it through `content_open` or switch **this session's** model selection — the line says explicitly not to edit the global chain for that, because a live probe watched the model do exactly that and drop the operator's other chain entries with it. Whole-chain AND (`image_multimodal_chain_decision`) is display-only — it feeds the `model_config` write echo and the group dialog's 「组内 N 颗不收图」 line, and it must never gate assembly again: gating on it makes an image-capable chain head blind because some later member is text-only. Off keeps uploads on the text downgrade path; on lets only the current visible turn's live request carry provider-visible image input, while durable transcript/baseline lanes strip the image blocks back out (which is why later turns never carry stale pixels).
- **Position inside the body is not configurable, and it is the question an operator cannot see from the config面.** A frontdoor request body is `system / 长期记忆 / user 轮 / 当轮运行时契约块` — the contract block is appended *after* the user turn, so an inbound upload's image block is never the last message. Most gateways read image blocks at any position; some read only the **last message's** blocks and silently ignore earlier ones (measured on `hy4-preview` through the local WorkBuddy bridge: the request is accepted, image tokens are not billed, and the model reports receiving no picture — there is no failure to learn from). The runtime supports the common shape only. The two preflight reads tell those questions apart: `image_count` says the body carries pixels, `image_blocks_at_tail` says whether pixels sit on the last message. For an upstream that reads only the last message, the way to hand it pixels is `content_open`, whose tail carrier puts them there; the model's own account is not evidence — a measured turn showed it asserting `image_multimodal_enabled=false` while its context contained no such field at all, the belief coming from a stale long-term-memory rule rather than from the config.
- The tail carrier used by `content_open` folds, never replaces. Its first text block carries the previous tail text (the runtime contract block) followed by the carrier's own note, so the hop that receives pixels still receives its `callable_tools` declaration; the node loop emits the same shape. Replacing instead would silently strip the runtime tool contract for one hop. It lives one hop: `_frontdoor_send_preflight_snapshot` attaches it after the contract is finalised and `_graph_call_model` clears the queue when the hop ends.
- A stable reopen lane, `attachment_reopen_targets`, rebuilt from current-turn uploads and earlier transcript metadata, lets uploaded files/images be reopened after the user text is rewritten into a direct-visual note. Voice clips are deliberately excluded (`kind=audio`): their transcript already travels in the message body, and a clip handle only invites the model to decode a binary it cannot read (see `speech-to-text.md` §3). Detached task creation does not auto-fill from this lane; `create_async_task.file_targets.path` must already be the exact absolute path of an existing file — the anchored relative forms the filesystem and content tools accept are deliberately not available here, because a detached task has no single owning temp directory (see `tool-and-skill-system.md`「Model Path Anchoring Contract」).
- Upload protection is two-layered: `/api/ceo/uploads` rejects a single image larger than `5 MiB`, and the runtime rechecks size before expanding an upload into a provider request, so bypassing the endpoint does not bypass the limit. The upload expansion and the reopen carrier share one check helper (`_checked_web_ceo_image_upload`), so a missing or oversized image fails identically whichever lane carries it.
- Upload lanes converge at one choke before the turn body is finalised: `_degrade_image_content_for_request` rewrites image blocks into the path-and-reason text whenever the judged model cannot take images, so **both** the web upload lane (which builds that text itself) and the external / MCP attachment lane (which injects `image_url` blocks straight into the message) end up with the same body that gets sent. This ordering is what keeps the request artifact and `image_count` honest — degrading only at the provider throat would leave an artifact claiming `image_count=1` for a turn that actually sent text. The external / MCP image block carries only a `data:` URL, so the choke recovers the on-disk path from the same message's attachment-note lines (`- image: … (local path: …)`), pairing them in document order; pairing is dropped and the `(inline)` placeholder kept whenever the counts disagree, because attaching path A to image B is worse than admitting the path is unknown.
- Historical reopen via `content_open` is a separate lane that injects a single-send runtime overlay plus the provider-visible image block; the overlay does not survive a later overflow, compression, or error, and a model config without the flag **fails with a tool error that names that config** rather than degrading to a text preview — the picture is already on disk, so the useful signal is "which config to switch to", not a silent empty result.

### 2.6. Attachment Bubble Rendering Contract

- Browser-side CEO message rendering must treat uploaded attachments as structured UI, not as plain transcript text.
- When a user message contains both text and attachments, the frontend should render:
  - the user text as the normal user text bubble
  - the attachments as separate attachment bubbles stacked directly below that text bubble in the same message lane
- When a user message contains attachments but no user-visible text, the frontend must render only the attachment bubbles. It must not synthesize a summary bubble such as `已附加附件`, and it must not surface the backend/internal `Uploaded attachments: ... local path ...` note as chat content after refresh or reconnect.
- The backend snapshot contract behind that rule is:
  - transcript/runtime messages may still carry the internal upload note in `content` for model/debugging purposes
  - `snapshot.ceo.messages[].content` must prefer `metadata.web_ceo_raw_text` whenever `metadata.web_ceo_uploads` is present, even when that raw text is the empty string
  - the authoritative user-facing attachment lane comes from `attachments`, not from reparsing the internal note text
  - that same persisted metadata lane is the source of truth for rebuilding `attachment_reopen_targets` in later frontdoor turns; if reopen paths break while attachment bubbles still restore, debug transcript metadata preservation first
- Non-image attachments should render as clickable file bubbles rather than inline text. Their click target is the in-app media surface, and the bytes still come from the backend-owned read-only file route `GET /api/ceo/uploads/file`, never from a raw local filesystem path — see「In-App Media Viewer And Download Lane」for what each file kind does there.
- Image attachments should render as thumbnail bubbles, not as ordinary file pills. The same thumbnail is the click target that opens the original in the in-app media surface (same pointer), and it carries no new-window semantics.
- `kind == 'audio'` is a third shape and never a pill: the user message itself becomes a voice bar (play/pause control, duration, and a `T` button at the right end whose `aria-label` is 转文字). Duration comes from the browser's `loadedmetadata`; `T` reveals the marker-stripped transcript **below the bar**, never inside it, and the `用户语音，机器识别结果：` line is stripped from the visible text on every user row — the marker exists for the model, and a bubble that prints it reads as the machine talking. The disclosure is wired through `.message-stack` because the button lives in the bar and the panel lives under it. The clip's own storage/visibility contract (decoded WAV on the wire, compressed to MP3 at the store seam with a WAV fallback, excluded from every model-visible surface, removed together with the session) is owned by `speech-to-text.md`「语音气泡与音频字段的存放」.
- Channel-session clips reach the same renderer through `metadata.external_attachments` (the bridge posts them over `/api/v1`); `_normalize_snapshot_attachments` lifts audio entries only — lifting every channel file would silently reshape how existing channel sessions display — and points them at `GET /api/ceo/external-upload-file?session_id=&path=`, a read route whose containment is that session's own `.g3ku/external-uploads/<slug>` directory (400 outside it, 400 on an empty `session_id`, 404 when the file is gone). It exists because `uploads/file` is rooted at the web upload directory and cannot serve a channel path.
- If refresh/reconnect shows the internal upload note instead of attachment bubbles, debug the snapshot builder in `g3ku/runtime/api/websocket_ceo.py` before debugging CSS or DOM layout.

### 2.7. Inline Markdown Image Rendering And Media Middle Layer

- The chat markdown renderer (`renderInlineMarkdown` in `g3ku/web/frontend/org_graph_app.js`) renders `![desc](src)` as an inline image for both persisted history and live turns: `https`/`http` URLs render directly, local filesystem paths resolve to the read-only route `GET /api/ceo/uploads/file`, and `data:` URIs or unknown schemes render nothing. This inline path complements, rather than replaces, the structured attachment bubbles in section 2.6.
- That file route serves from two allowed roots only — the per-session upload directory and `workspace_path()/output` — returning `400 upload_path_outside_session_dir` for paths outside either root and `404` for missing files.
- Most assistant local references are rewritten at the snapshot egress by the media middle layer (`g3ku/runtime/api/ceo_media.py`) so they become servable without touching the on-disk transcript: raster images become staged thumbnails inside an already-allowed root, other local files become signed viewer URLs, and URLs, already-servable references, and failed rewrites pass through unchanged.
- Originals are reachable only through the signed viewer route `GET /api/ceo/media/original?token=...`, which takes an unguessable signed token rather than a caller-chosen path. Because only raster image bytes are ever staged into a serving root, the endpoint cannot become an arbitrary file oracle.

### 2.8. Message Edit-Resend And Session Fork Contract

两个动作都挂在各自气泡的元信息行里，没有第三种"操作行"：`编辑` 在**用户气泡**那一行的最右（发送时间右边），`Fork` 在**模型回复**那一行的最左（`输入 · 缓存命中 · 输出 · 轮数 · 完成于 …` 那段文字的左边）。轨道回合那一行是 `.ceo-turn-meta`，普通气泡是 `.msg-meta`，两颗按钮都常驻；同一行那些读数按点击才显形（判据见「Per-Turn Token Usage Contract」）。背后是同一套服务端契约。Server-side core lives in `g3ku/runtime/web_ceo_history_edit.py`; endpoints hang off the existing `ceo_sessions` router.

- **Eligibility flags are server-owned, and the two actions have different axes.** `snapshot.ceo.messages[]` carries `can_edit_fork` on user items and `can_fork_reply` on assistant items（回复行那颗按钮，判据见下面「Fork 只有一个切点」条目），assistant items may also carry `task_dispatched`. 提问轴没有第三种 flag：`turn_id` 在提问行和它的回复行上是同一个值，共用一个键会让"这条提问可 Fork"冒充"上面那条回复之后可 Fork"，所以 Fork 资格只在 assistant 侧落键，用户行只带编辑资格。Both share the content gate; only edit adds a runtime gate, because edit rewrites the source transcript while fork is a read-only prefix copy:
  - content gate (both actions): the session is a local `web:` session (channel sessions never get flags); async-task gate — truncation deletes `[clicked message, transcript end]`, so only a task whose **first dispatch falls inside that range and is still unfinished** closes the gate. The first appearance of a `task:` id on an assistant transcript row (`metadata.task_ids`) is the dispatch point: echo rows — a heartbeat reply that merely mentions a task number, and the `manual_pause_archive` / `follow_up_archive` rows that re-stamp ids read from the canonical context — repeat an id already seen and therefore never close it, and a task established before the boundary keeps its dispatch record in the retained prefix, so it never closes the gate either. Unfinished is read from `list_unfinished_tasks_for_session` (task `status == 'in_progress'`, which also covers a paused task tree); when that read is unavailable the gate treats every in-range first dispatch as unfinished. Legacy transcripts without any `task_ids` field compare unfinished-task `created_at` against the clicked message's own `timestamp`; the message is the first of its user-run (a maximal consecutive run of visible user messages — prompt-batch siblings and mid-turn consumed follow-ups share one execution turn and have no clean boundary, so only the run head is actionable; truncating the run head removes the whole run); a per-turn boundary snapshot exists for the run's anchor turn, or the boundary is the session start. The anchor is the row physically before the run head, walked back across internal-turn rows (heartbeat/cron replies and internal prompt rows never write a snapshot, so they cannot evict a user turn's) and resolved through `archived_from_turn_id` when that row is an archive row (a `follow_up_archive` row's compound `<turn>:followup:<id>` turn id is never a snapshot key). Snapshots live at `.g3ku/web-ceo-turn-boundaries/<session>/<turn_id>.json.gz` (gzip, most recent `TURN_BOUNDARY_SNAPSHOT_KEEP` = 12 per session, user turns only). There is no heuristic rebuild: messages older than the retained window are permanently ineligible.
  - runtime gate (edit only): the session is fully stable — no inflight/preserved turn (including heartbeat/cron internal turns), no queued follow-ups, no pending approval interrupts, no compression in flight. **`status == "paused"` / `paused == True` / `status == "error"` are stable, not unstable**: the criterion is "does the source session still have a live executor", and once `is_running` is false a paused or failed turn has stopped — truncating its transcript is safe. Rejecting `status == "running"` and `paused` here is a defect, not a safety margin: after a manual pause or a runtime error the flags vanish (and `_assert_edit_fork_runtime_idle` answers 409), leaving the user unable to edit the very message they just paused. 等审批是唯一的例外，而且它由 `pending_interrupts` 拦，不由 `paused` 拦——那一轮还没结束，截断会连同待审批调用一起切掉。
  - The frontend mirrors each axis on its own side: the feed-level `.ceo-turn-active` class hides the edit button, edit clicks carry a toast guard, and fork clicks are guarded only by the upload/pause/catalog busy flags — the reply row's Fork sits outside that class's reach, which is what keeps Fork clickable while a turn runs, is paused, or waits on approval. `.ceo-turn-active` follows `S.ceoTurnActive`, which is `false` again once the session settles, so a paused turn's edit button needs no CSS exemption. 点击守卫 `ceoHistoryEditBusyReason` 也必须用现算判据（`activeCeoSessionIsNonStable`），不能读那个布尔量——理由见「Active-Turn Button Semantics」§1.1。
- **Flag delivery has two frames, both from the same gate call.** `snapshot.ceo` carries per-row flags at connect; `ceo.edit_fork.gates {turn_ids, fork_reply_turn_ids}` re-delivers both gate sets on every CEO websocket `state_snapshot`, including the ones reporting `paused` — a blocked session has no usable composer, so the reply row's Fork is the only exit left. The second frame exists because eligibility moves at turn end: the edit gate needs the inflight sidecar gone, and this run's own dispatch row either appears (revoking the flag while that task is unfinished) or settles without one (granting it) only by finalize — `_emit_state_snapshot` re-syncs that sidecar right before emitting, which is why the frame hangs off `state_snapshot` and not `message_end` (at `message_end` the sidecar still carries the running turn). Each list holds the run-head keys of its own gated rows, so the browser flags the first cached row **of that list's own role** per turn id (`turn_ids` 落用户行，`fork_reply_turn_ids` 落助手行；prompt-batch siblings share one id), revokes every other row, then re-renders through `renderCeoSnapshot`; the render signature covers both flags, so an unchanged set costs no rebuild. Gated transcript rows without a turn id are skipped — the turn id is the button key. Channel sessions never receive the frame. 提问轴那份列表随 `can_fork` 一起删掉：`fork_gates`（只看内容的门槛）仍然照算，但只作为反推 `fork_reply_turn_ids` 的输入存在，不单独下发。
- **A gates frame can only flag a row the browser already has in its cached transcript.** Rows a turn leaves behind normally arrive inside `ceo.reply.final` (`finalizeCeoTurn` writes them into the snapshot cache). A manual pause ends without a final frame — the server archives the run into the transcript and clears the inflight sidecar — so `finalizePausedCeoTurn` lands that run's user row **plus** its `已暂停` assistant row through the same `buildFinalizedCeoTurnPayload` the final path uses. 这段收尾有四条不变量：

  - **两半各自独立。** live 元素可能已经不在（心跳内部轮、或那一跳在别处被摘掉），但缓存补行必须照做；反过来，补行失败也不能让已经画好的气泡丢掉「已暂停」。所以「气泡就地定稿」与「缓存补转录行」分开执行，谁也不早退谁。
  - **两行都要 `turn_id`。** 用户行的 id 从在飞 lane 认领（`normalizeCeoSnapshotInflight` 只留 content/timestamp），回复行的 id 由 `buildFinalizedCeoTurnPayload` 的 `normalizedTurnId` 写上。缺了它，门槛帧按 `turn_id` 匹配永远盖不上章——编辑按钮与回复行 Fork 都只能等刷新。
  - **回合轨道要带住。** `finalTraceContext` 优先取 live 回合自己渲染出的 `lastExecutionTraceSummary`，取不到再回退 lane 的 `turns`。不带轨道时，随后那次门槛帧触发的整页重建会画出一条没有工具组的暂停气泡，与暂停瞬间看到的两样。
  - **状态要写成 `paused`。** `buildFinalizedCeoTurnPayload` 的 `status` 参数让缓存行与刷新后服务端归档行同形（`status=paused`）；不带时同一回合在暂停后与刷新后是两种画法。

  这四条不变量缺一条，就会退回「暂停后气泡消失、要刷新才回来」或「暂停了却没有编辑按钮」。

  A pause caused by a pending approval interrupt is not a finished turn and must not write transcript rows: the lane stays alive and resumes into the same turn.
- **Edit-resend** = `POST /api/ceo/sessions/{id}/truncate` `{turn_id}` then the ordinary WS send. The endpoint truncates transcript from the run head (atomic JSONL rewrite, `last_user_turn_at`/`commit_turn_counter`/preview recomputed), replaces the continuity sidecar with the prev-turn boundary snapshot (`continuity_source=turn_boundary`) or an empty baseline at session start (`fresh_path`), clears inflight/paused sidecars, deletes removed turns' actual-request artifacts (mandatory for `fresh_path`: an empty-baseline sidecar cannot stop the restart artifact fallback from resurrecting deleted turns), and mutates the live `RuntimeAgentSession` in place (`apply_history_truncation_state`) so stale WS closures cannot reuse the old baseline. On `fresh_path` that in-place reset also applies the empty payload's tool lists (`provider_tool_schema_names` / `hydrated_tool_names` / `visible_tool_ids`) and drops the pinned head through `drop_pinned_contract_state`, which clears both carriers (session attributes plus the per-`session_key` process table) — the first post-edit hop therefore re-seeds `tools[]` from that round's RBAC-visible set instead of continuing to send the pre-truncation bundle (contract in `tool-and-skill-system.md`「Provider Tool Surface」). The frontend then closes the WS, clears local follow-up chips and the session snapshot cache, reconnects, waits for open (`whenCeoWsOpen`), and sends the edited text/uploads through the existing `client.user_message` lane — the single send pipeline owns streaming/finalize. The composer shows a warning banner while in edit mode (`S.ceoEditResend`); cancel restores the previous draft. Truncation is irreversible by design: there is no backup or undo.
- **Fork** = `POST /api/ceo/sessions/{id}/fork` `{turn_id, title?}`，`turn_id` 指**那条模型回复所在的轮次**（请求体里没有第二种切点参数：`at` 与提问轴一起摘掉了，判据见下一条）。Read-only on the source session (safe while a source turn runs; the boundary snapshot is read into memory before copying). The new session gets: the transcript prefix (deep-copied rows), attachment files copied into the new session's upload dir with descriptor/`attachments`/content-note and baseline path rewrites (shared paths would fail the per-session `uploads/file` root check), the truncated continuity sidecar, and a copy of the prev-turn boundary snapshot (so the fork's tail stays editable). Task records never migrate. The response is create-shaped catalog payload plus `fork.composer {text, uploads}`; the frontend activates the new session and prefills the composer through the draft lane without auto-sending. 成功侧不弹 toast——切好的会话与输入框里的原文就是回执，浮层只会盖住刚切好的会话列表；失败侧必须带 `editForkErrorText` 的错误提示，因为那一步什么都没换。
- **Fork 只有一个切点：那条回复之后。** `reply_fork_target` 找该轮最后一条可见回复（静默行、内部轮行、空正文行都不算），再往后找第一条可见用户消息，把它的 `turn_id` 交给边界模型——前缀、连续性锚点、保留窗口、任务门槛全部复用编辑重发那条既有路径，不会出现"按物理下标切"与快照不一致（中间夹过心跳轮时，那条心跳的效果本来就在下一条提问的快照里）。**找不到就是尾部**：整份转录进新会话、连续性取源会话当前的 completed sidecar（`continuity_source=session_continuity`）、composer 留空，所以最后一条回复也有 Fork 入口，不必等下一条提问发出来；尾部不截任何东西，因此任务门槛与 12 轮窗口都不适用。回复行资格由 `compute_reply_fork_turn_ids` 从同一份内容门槛反推（有资格提问的上一条可见回复）再加上尾部那条，所以门槛判据只有一套实现。
- **Validation and concurrency**: both endpoints re-run the content gate server-side (`turn_not_found` 404; `turn_not_run_first` / `boundary_unavailable` / `edit_fork_blocked_by_async_task` / `channel_session_readonly` 409; `no_model_configured` 503). Runtime idleness is checked where a write could be overwritten by a finishing turn: truncate calls `_assert_edit_fork_runtime_idle` (`ceo_turn_in_progress` 409) before and inside the session `_turn_lock` (5s acquire timeout, closing the heartbeat race), deletion gates the same state before its first destructive step (见「Channel Session Clear Contract」), while `fork_ceo_session` inspects no runtime state and takes no lock — that asymmetry is the contract, not a missed check. `_assert_edit_fork_runtime_idle` 与快照侧总开关**同一语义**：拦 `is_running` / 待审批 / 排队补充 / 压缩在途，放行 paused、error、idle、completed——两处必须一起改，只改一处就是「按钮看得见但点下去 409」或反过来。
- **Known edges**: bubbles appended by the live send path carry no flag of their own — both action rows arrive with the gates frame, and that frame reaches only rows already in the cached transcript, so a still-running turn shows neither 编辑 on its user row nor Fork on its reply row until finalize writes those rows into the cache (the live lane's elements are not cached rows); a task first established after the boundary closes the gate while it is unfinished and reopens it once the task reaches a terminal state — truncating a finished task's dispatch record is accepted because nothing writes back to it any more; a task id that the transcript references but the task store does not have is treated as finished (deleted tasks leave such ids behind); the first post-edit request pays a one-time prompt-cache miss; the memory review window (`memory/review_state.json`) has no per-turn pruning, so buffered review records of removed turns may still flush to the advisory memory lane later (conversation context is unaffected); `user_edit_truncation` is a whitelisted continuity shrink/source reason (forensics in `context-and-cache-troubleshooting.md`).

### 2.9. CEO Websocket Lane Failure Contract

`/ws/ceo` 的失败面只有一个方向会致命：车道静默而 socket 还开着。此时浏览器既收不到后续帧，也不会触发 `onclose` 的 1 秒自动重连，界面就永久停在半截回合（转圈、没有最终文案），只有手动刷新才同步——转录里通常早已落好完整 assistant 行，所以这属展示层车道故障，不是数据缺失。

- 三条 `sender()` 队列与连接期内联发送共用一条 socket，因此**任意时刻只允许一个写者**：所有出口写都走 `_safe_send`，它在连接期一把 `asyncio.Lock` 下串行。`websockets` legacy 协议明确不支持并发 `send()`——并发会在那条链路上抛 `AssertionError`（`_drain_helper`），帧被吞掉而 socket 仍开着，正是下面描述的静默形状（`console.log` 里按 `CEO websocket failed to send frame` 逐帧计数可证）。新增第二条写路径必须复用同一把锁。
- 单帧写失败（载荷不可序列化、半开连接）只丢那一帧并记 `logger.exception`；连续 `_SENDER_CONSECUTIVE_FAILURE_LIMIT`（3）帧都失败才判定链路已废：置 `closed`、以 code 1011 主动关掉 socket，让前端走 `onclose` 重连重取快照。sender 静默退出等于把一次可恢复故障变成永久静默。
- 转发层 `relay_session_event` 是 `_relay_session_event` 的守卫包装：`RuntimeAgentSession._emit` 逐个 `await` 订阅者且不做捕获，转发层抛出异常会打断正在收尾的回合（`message_end` 之后还有 `state_snapshot`/`turn_end`），前端因此连权威收尾帧都等不到。守卫只记日志不上抛，`CancelledError` 继续传播。
- 浏览器只接受 JSON 帧：`onmessage` 的 `JSON.parse` 必须带 try/catch。裸解析下，一帧解析失败会让该 socket 之后每帧都被同一个异常吞掉。失败即 `closeCeoWs()` + `initCeoWs()` 重连重取快照，并按 `CEO_WS_PARSE_RESYNC_LIMIT` 限量（收到 `snapshot.ceo` 即恢复预算），避免解析不了时打成重连风暴。

### 2.10. Live Reasoning Row Contract

- "回合卡死"的实测形状是解码量大而可见产出为零：`web:ceo-28ce3df601a5` 那一跳 `completion_tokens=3037`，其中 2,591 个是思考 token，正文一个字都没进气泡，而速率、队列、工具、压缩、重试全部正常——慢的是不可见的解码。进行中的加载行因此不只写 `正在思考...`：加载图标右侧报本回合累计耗时，耗时右侧报思考 token，再右侧显示思考内容的最后一行，**点这一行本身**才展开成固定高度的滚动窗。
- 该行走专用事件 `ceo.reasoning.delta`，载荷 `{turn_id, source, attempt, seq, delta, chars, est_tokens, seconds}`。`delta` 是 append-only 增量，**不是** `ceo.reply.delta` 那种"整段权威覆盖"：一跳思考实测 8.6–9.5K 字符、约 1,100 个流分片，按正文那套整段口径转发会把一个回合推到 MB 级（同一回合按增量帧实测 ≈87 KB）。新帧沿用同一条 `stream_queue` 与 `_safe_send`，不另开写者（写者唯一性的合同见「CEO Websocket Lane Failure Contract」）。
- 每帧都带 `chars` / `est_tokens` / `seconds` 三个**累计**标量，浏览器不自己累加，丢帧或重连后下一帧即自洽。`seq` 单调，`seq` 不增的帧被前端拒绝；`attempt` 是 provider attempt 段号，段号变了整段重来——同跳重发与换链降级都会重放整条思考，把它当追加就是正文车道上已经赔过的"叠两份"。
- 上游侧：`g3ku/providers/streaming_timeouts.py` 在 `reasoning_content` 与 `reasoning` 两种方言上取分片，经 `on_reasoning_delta` 回调出来。它**绝不**借用正文的 `on_text_delta`：后者会把 `attempt_visible_text_streamed` 置真，而该标记决定流中断时的重试与故障归类（`StreamIncomplete`），借道等于让一次纯思考的半截流被误判成"已经吐过正文"。能力位 `supports_reasoning_delta_streaming`（`providers/base.py` 默认 `False`，`openai_chat_provider.py` 为 `True`）与 `supports_streaming` 双门控，不支持的 provider 就是不发帧，不留分支；非流式与 `_DirectProviderChatBackend` 那条直连道照旧吞掉回调，和它们吞正文回调同形。
- Responses 车道这一行只有耗时与 token 数：**协议本身没有 reasoning 增量事件**，思考以 `type="reasoning"` 输出项在 `response.output_item.done` / `response.completed` 边界整项交付（合同见 `runtime-overview.md`「思考内容（reasoning）的上下文回放」），没有可转发的分片。这是协议性质，不是本项目实现缺口。
- 计时与体量按**回合**、段落按 **attempt**：`_begin_assistant_reasoning_segment()` 在每次 provider attempt 的起点先把上一发的耗时与体量收进它自己所属的回合，`_reasoning_touch_turn()` 再以 `turn_id` 为界把 `_reasoning_turn_seconds` / `_reasoning_chars_total` / `_reasoning_est_tokens_total` 一并清零。界面上那一行因此报的是**本回合**累计——多跳回合（心跳、工具轮）各跳都算进来，既不是只有最后一跳，也不是整条会话的总量；收口与划界的先后顺序就是这条不变量的实现点。
- 每一段的窗口是**该跳请求起点 → 最后一个思维块到达**（`_reasoning_last_delta_monotonic` 随每个思维块前推，`_reasoning_seconds()` 读它而不是读 `now`）：思维吐完之后模型还在写工具调用参数（写文件时是一整个文件正文），以及正文流式、工具执行，都不算思考；一个思维块都没到的跳贡献 0 秒。`_reasoning_close_segment()` 的两个调用点——该跳正文首帧、该跳模型调用返回——只负责收口内容与停段，读数不会走到末块时刻之后。排队/预填充在该跳有思维块时算进这一行（单块交付的车道只有这一段可归因）。
- 表只覆盖思考发生的窗口，收口点有三个：本跳**正文首帧**（`_handle_assistant_text_delta` 打开正文段时）、`_graph_call_model` 里**模型调用返回之后**（工具执行在其后开始）、以及下一次 attempt 起点（兜住前两处都没走到的路径）。这条判据不能只靠 attempt 起点：每一次 `state_snapshot` 都会重算 `_reasoning_running_totals()` 并把标量送进快照，前端 `applyCeoThinkingFromInflightSnapshot` 据此重画那一行，于是表没停多久、界面就多报多久——工具跑三十秒，`思考 Ns` 就跟着涨三十秒。收口是幂等的（`_reasoning_started_monotonic` 归零后重算即得 0），三处重复调用只加一次时长。
- token 读数的诚实性：进行中一律带 `~`，因为它是按字符估算——provider 的 usage 只在流尾给，且不少网关连 `completion_tokens_details.reasoning_tokens` 都不报（实测本地 WorkBuddy 桥对 243 字符的思考回 `reasoning_tokens: 0`）。只有真值到手那一刻才去掉 `~`：回合收尾时把 `turn_usage.thinking_tokens` 并进转录行（`assistant_payload["thinking"].tokens`），历史行按它渲染。真值的载体是回合 usage 累加器 `_frontdoor_turn_usage[turn_id]`：写侧只在 provider 上报这一项时累加，读侧（`_turn_usage_payload`，以及续跑轮认领累计器时读的工件聚合 `read_session_turn_token_usage`）按同一条判据带出该键——没上报就缺键，缺键与 0 的区分不能在任何一层被补成 0。上报值是 0 时那一行仍留 `~`：0 与成段思考同时出现说明网关没算这一项，估算比假真值诚实。
